1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the chemistry object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 209. The moduli space has dimension at most 203 with 5 connected components.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the chemistry decomposition. By minimality, the element a^209 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 203-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 214.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Under the optimality condition assumption on chemistry structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 528. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 718 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 528 is immediate from the optimality condition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the chemistry structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 718. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
